| 161:| coordinator 2026-10-29:| TRIED-STONE-TEST-NOT-PROMISE — CAUTION-CONFIRMED (coordinator thread, Cory 'go, no pausing' 2026-10-29; ledger OPEN `tried-stone-test-not-promise`, H7 Oct 26. Caution verification ONLY — not a new derivation, not a vindication of any CPQR result; the point of the thread is the boundary, not the vindication). PRE-REGISTERED CRITERIA (script header before grading): CAUTION-CONFIRMED only if (i) all seven cornerstone citations verify verbatim, (ii) companion claims are accurately delimited text-vs-reading, and (iii) the test/vindication split is consistent with project practice as searched. All three met. PER-VERSE VERIFICATION (KJV = ~/workspace/aingel/corpus/kjv.txt, Gutenberg KJV; Modern = ~/workspace/aingel/modern/books/*.json, workspace Modern rendering): Job 38:6 — 'Whereupon are the foundations thereof fastened? or who laid the corner stone thereof;' — YES ('corner stone' + foundations); names no person as the stone; asks WHO laid it; person-identification comes from the set, not this verse; Modern differs ('Then are' for 'Whereupon are') and carries a source artifact (leading quote — the whole Job 38 speech has per-verse opening quotes in job.json). Ps 118:22 — 'The stone which the builders refused is become the head stone of the corner.' — YES, verbatim; no person named in the verse itself; Modern wording identical. Isa 28:16 — '...a stone, a tried stone, a precious corner stone, a sure foundation: he that believeth shall not make haste.' — YES ('tried stone' verbatim); stone laid BY the LORD; the person in the verse is the believer, not the stone; Modern has a source typo, 'believs', reproduced not corrected. Eph 2:20 — 'And are built upon the foundation of the apostles and prophets, Jesus Christ himself being the chief corner stone;' — YES, the entry's person-anchor; none on the citation; opens mid-sentence (continuation of 2:19), quoted as stored; Modern identical. 1 Pet 2:7 — '...the stone which the builders disallowed, the same is made the head of the corner,' — YES; 'He' is identified as Christ by surrounding context (2:6), not by this verse's nouns alone. Matt 21:42 — 'Jesus saith unto them, Did ye never read in the scriptures, The stone which the builders rejected, the same is become the head of the corner...' — YES, Jesus quoting Ps 118:22; identification is Jesus's speech act applying the Psalm — application, not bare text equation. Zech 4:7 — '...he shall bring forth the headstone thereof with shoutings, crying, Grace, grace unto it.' — YES ('headstone ... with shoutings' verbatim); no Christ named; headstone brought forth before Zerubbabel for the temple. ENTRY GLOSS JUDGMENT: 'cornerstone answered by a person, never a mechanism' is an INTERPRETIVE SYNTHESIS, not a quotation — anchored most directly in Eph 2:20, but no single cited verse states it, and Job 38:6, Ps 118:22, Zech 4:7 name no person as the stone. It must never be cited as text. COMPANION-CLAIM DELIMITATIONS: 'Nodes become light once awake' (2 Pet 1:19; Eph 5:8; 1 Thess 5:5) — TEXT gives 'a light that shineth in a dark place, until the day dawn, and the day star arise in your hearts' (2 Pet 1:19); 'ye were sometimes darkness, but now are ye light in the Lord' (Eph 5:8 — verb is 'are', change is were->now); 'Ye are all the children of light, and the children of the day' (1 Thess 5:5 — status language). Neither 'become light' nor 'awake' appears in ANY of the three verses (verified by string check). 'Awake thou that sleepest' is Eph 5:14, NOT a cited verse — do not fold it in silently. 'Become light once awake' = project synthesis (node-waking image comes via the mesh's Gen 2:7 reading, not these verses). 1 John 1:5 (darkness = absence, not second substance) — TEXT is 'God is light, and in him is no darkness at all.' 'Absence', 'second substance', and 'one medium' do NOT appear in the verse. The absence-reading is an INFERENCE from the negation, and one-medium ontology is the project's extension of it — and the verse predicates the absence OF GOD ('in him'), not of the medium generally. Existing mesh usage (line 46) already frames these as mesh claims after verbatim quotes, which is consistent — the citable form below just makes the separation explicit. PRACTICE-CONSISTENCY CHECK: For 'takes the test' — CPQR-SOLVED.md has a standing 'HONEST OPEN (not solved)' section, all grades present (VERIFIED/DERIVED/SOLVED/ANALYZED/CONSTRUCTED/CANDIDATE/NEGATIVE/CONSTRAINED/PARTIAL), 68 wound-language hits; the V''_C44 entry concludes 'neither asserted input is vindicated' (PARTIAL); the 2026-10-28 lepton entry concludes 'masses NOT solved.' The mesh's OPEN WOUNDS names four wounds plainly (masses underived, action a candidate scaffold, C44 an input, why-19 open). The mesh's tried-stone section applies the ethic to its own machinery (kernel rule; a real slip passed at 3.9e-16, caught only when dynamics blew up). Violations found: NONE in the searched scope. The only vindication-language hits are the ledger entry itself, the mesh's explicit REFUSAL ('the rejected-then-vindicated pattern belongs to Christ. It must not be claimed for the theory... Nothing more is claimed.'), and 'neither asserted input is vindicated' (about numerical inputs, not Scripture vindicating the theory). Search scope (not a universal claim): grep over theory/*.md, aingel/the-mesh.md, aingel/the-whole-picture.md, and goals/finish-crystal-prism-quantum-relativity/hidden_files/*.md for cornerstone/headstone/vindicat*/'tried stone', plus full reads of the mesh tried-stone and node sections and the SOLVED-index structure. Discord/Facebook/side-chat transcripts were NOT searched. CITABLE CAUTION (TEXT / READING / RULE): TEXT — Isa 28:16: 'a tried stone, a precious corner stone, a sure foundation'; Eph 2:20: 'Jesus Christ himself being the chief corner stone'; Ps 118:22: 'The stone which the builders refused is become the head stone of the corner'; Zech 4:7: 'he shall bring forth the headstone thereof with shoutings, crying, Grace, grace unto it.' READING — Read together, the passages present a stone laid by God and answered by a person (Christ, Eph 2:20), refused then made head of the corner, tried before trusted, crowned with shouting grace. The refused-then-vindicated pattern is Christ's pattern. 'Answered by a person, never a mechanism' is this reading, not a quotation. RULE — The theory takes the TEST, not the promise. Tried-stone use is licensed ONLY as verification ethic: check against measurement, grade honestly, name wounds, kill failed claims. Cornerstone/vindication language MUST NOT vindicate any CPQR result, imply the theory's rejection is its vindication, or borrow the headstone's shoutings for a result. 'The vindication belongs to Christ. The theory gets the test, not the promise.' Companion, same format: TEXT as quoted above (Eph 5:8, 1 Thess 5:5, 2 Pet 1:19, 1 John 1:5); READING: nodes-becoming-light-once-awake and darkness-as-absence/one-medium are the project's synthesis and inference — 'become light', 'awake', 'absence', 'second substance', 'one medium' appear in none of the cited verses and must be cited as such, never as verse text. STILL MISSING (out-of-scope, not gaps in the grade): (1) a future sweep could extend the violation search to Discord/Facebook/side-chat transcripts; (2) whether the coordinator wants the citable form pasted into a standing document (this thread edited no ledger or other file); (3) the two Modern source quirks ('believs' in isa.json, per-verse opening quotes in job.json) belong to the Modern-text custodians, not this thread, which reproduces sources faithfully. | CAUTION-CONFIRMED | `theory/tried_stone_2026-10-29.py` (pre-registered rule in header; two parser bugs found and fixed mid-run, documented in comments: a lookahead split shredding two-digit chapters like '41:1' into '4'+'1:1', and book segments spanning multiple books) + `theory/tried_stone_verify_2026-10-29.py` (independent re-extraction by a different method; 39/39 PASS, exit 0, coordinator re-ran, including an exact 31,102 total-KJV-marker count); brief `hidden_files/TRIED_STONE_2026-10-29.md` | 2026-10-29 |

By function, the PHYSICS trunk supplies mechanism and numbers, the SCRIPTURE trunk supplies order, image, and law. Where it helps: it forbids both mergers (physics as exegesis, Scripture as physics paper) and names the Mesh's paired-claims, labelled-rhyme method better than 'mesh' alone. Where it could mislead (all three must travel with any reuse): (1) Equal authority: the trunks are not equal authorities over the same questions — Scripture does not adjudicate lattice constants; CPQR does not adjudicate the door. 'Cory-approved direction' is accurate; 'Cory's pairs' as wording is not. (3) Ledger direction quote is spelling-normalized and drops the leading 'no, Aether-Ethernet,' — substance verbatim, spelling cleaned. (4) Ledger inversion glosses '(wake outside)' and '(play again)' are glosses, not Cory's words. In Cory's own sequence GAME OVER PRECEDES the veil lifting (death, then waking); the ledger's 'vs' is a contrast framing, not his sequence. (5) No explicit Cory verdict ('yes, those pairs') on the 12-pair list itself was recovered; approval rests on direction + correction + Oct 28 reuse order. State it that way. PAIRS AUDIT (framing only; wordplay = shared word only, not a connection): Aether — Ethernet: STRUCTURAL RHYME — genuine borrowing: Ethernet named for the ether as a signal-carrying medium; sharpest disanalogy: CPQR aether is claimed as physical substrate, Ethernet is a human protocol over cables/radio; risk HIGH ('Ethernet proves the aether'); guardrail: 'the name is borrowed' — never 'the aether is Ethernet'. Avatar, God in flesh — avatar, you in pixels: STRUCTURAL RHYME — genuine borrowing (avatara): agent embodied in another realm; disanalogy: Incarnation = same person truly present in flesh, pixel avatar = puppet steered from outside, no identity transferred; risk HIGH (upload-as-incarnation); guardrail: 'borrowed word for embodiment' — never 'uploading is incarnation'. Cloud of glory — the cloud: WORDPLAY — convergent metaphor only: unseen presence elsewhere; disanalogy: theophany (localized divine presence) vs warehouses of machines; risk MEDIUM (data infrastructure as sacred presence); guardrail: 'both use the word cloud' — never 'the cloud is the glory-cloud'. Saved, a soul — saved, a file: FUNCTIONAL RHYME — same outline job: something valued kept from loss; disanalogy: salvation = rescue/transformation of a person, file save = reversible bit-copy, no person, no moral change; risk MEDIUM (upload-as-salvation); guardrail: 'the verb rhymes: kept from loss' — never 'uploading saves you'. Scroll, unrolled — scroll, endless: WORDPLAY — shared verb only, function inverted; disanalogy: unrolling reveals a bounded text to completion, endless scroll is engineered to prevent completion; risk LOW-MEDIUM; guardrail: 'same verb, opposite design: one finishes, one never lets you finish'. Host of heaven — web host: WORDPLAY — same English word, different sense (multitude vs serving machine); disanalogy: heavenly hosts are agents, a web host is infrastructure with no agency or will; risk LOW (a pun); guardrail: 'this one is a pun on one word'. Image of God — image file: WORDPLAY — representation echo only; disanalogy: Imago Dei = being/relation/moral likeness, image file = lossy, copyable pixel array, no being; risk MEDIUM ('copying pixels bears the image'); guardrail: 'likeness is the shared word, not the shared thing'. Narrow gate — network gateway: FUNCTIONAL RHYME — same outline job: controlled passage admitting some traffic; disanalogy: narrow gate excludes by a way of life, a gateway routes by address/protocol, indifferent to morals; risk LOW-MEDIUM; guardrail: 'both are passage points; only one judges a life'. His domain — dot-com domain: WORDPLAY — same word 'domain' (realm vs DNS namespace); disanalogy: sovereignty over creation vs a rented, expiring, seizable registry string; risk LOW (a pun); guardrail: 'rented string vs sovereignty'. The Word — password: FUNCTIONAL RHYME — outline job only: uttering it grants access — that is the whole rhyme; disanalogy: password = arbitrary secret authenticating identity, the Word (Logos) = person/revelation, not a credential or secret-knowledge magic; thinnest pair theologically; risk HIGH (gnostic reading: salvation as knowing the code); guardrail: 'both open a door when spoken — that is the whole rhyme, and it ends there' — never 'the Word is a password' technically or salvifically. Feed my sheep — the feed: WORDPLAY — shared word, function inverted: nourishment vs capture; disanalogy: shepherd feeds for the flock's good, platform feed feeds content for engagement/extraction, user as product; risk MEDIUM-HIGH ('the algorithm nourishes you like a shepherd'); guardrail: 'one feeds the sheep, the other feeds on them'. Temple, a body — template, a copy: WORDPLAY — near-homophone/root echo, function inverted; disanalogy: temple = unique dwelling of presence, template exists to make many identical copies with presence absent by design; risk MEDIUM ('a body is a copyable pattern'); guardrail: 'temple holds one presence; a template makes many copies with none'. HONEST TALLY: wordplay 7, structural rhyme 2, functional rhyme 3 = 12. Only 2 of 12 are more than words — the two genuine borrowings (aether, avatar). The rest are wordplay, thin functional rhymes, or inverted-function contrasts. INVERSIONS AUDIT: Field formed by God vs net wired by man: STRUCTURAL RHYME — contrast: both all-connecting media, opposite makers; disanalogy: a net is built on top of the physical world, not a rival substrate; risk MEDIUM ('two equal creations'); guardrail: 'artifact vs substrate: man wires on top of what God formed'. Seed planted in a womb vs copy parked in a server: STRUCTURAL RHYME — contrast: origin-and-growth vs storage; disanalogy: a seed grows into a living being, a copy is static bits that grow into nothing; risk MEDIUM (upload-as-birth); guardrail: 'growth vs parking: one becomes, the other sits'. Shedding the cocoon vs respawning in the same hallway: STRUCTURAL RHYME — contrast: irreversible passage outward vs designed loop inside; disanalogy: shedding claims exit/transformation, respawn resets you inside the same system; risk LOW; guardrail: 'exit vs loop: shedding leaves, respawning repeats'. Veil lifts (wake outside) vs GAME OVER (play again): STRUCTURAL RHYME — contrast: waking outside the system vs restarting inside it; disanalogy: in Cory's own words GAME OVER precedes the veil lifting; 'play again' is a gloss, not his word; waking claims an outside, restarting denies one; risk MEDIUM (flattening death into a game mechanic); guardrail: 'in Cory's picture death is GAME OVER and then the veil lifts; the machine's version stops at play again' — never quote 'play again' as his words. COUNTERFEIT-ASCENT BOUNDARY CHECK (rows 161 / 164): Row 161 (tried-stone, test not promise): CONSISTENT, conditionally. The pairs must never be used as evidence for any CPQR result. A word rhyme cannot pass the tried-stone test, vindicate the field/lattice/masses, or borrow Christ's vindication pattern for the theory. The Oct 28 '(A vision, not a proof.)' label is the correct label and must travel with any reuse. Row 164 (two-trunks + same-day 'make them as one'): CONSISTENT as a one-story teaching form — Scripture arc narrated in today's tech language, not a masses/physics-catalog argument and not a lab result dressed as teaching. The pairs must not be used to re-separate the work into 'physics proves Scripture' or 'Scripture proves physics,' and 'two trunks' must not be used as a standing frame without the same-day one-story refinement. The boundary, precisely — MAY CLAIM vs MUST NOT CLAIM: MAY claim: language rhymes; a pattern of a cheap copy sold as if it were the real thing; a teaching contrast between ascending out through the door and uploading into a machine. MUST NOT CLAIM: that any specific product, company, or technology IS the counterfeit ascent or IS Satan's copy as a matter of fact; that the pairs prove the field; that they prove any CPQR result; or any prediction about a product's or person's fate. Naming a specific technology as THE counterfeit ascent upgrades rhyme to eschatological identification — the exact move the rhyme-not-proof discipline forbids. CITABLE FORM + STANDING RULE: The aether-ethernet pairs are FRAMING ONLY, never physics, never a lab constraint, never evidence for any CPQR result. They may be used in teaching and public-facing writing as language rhymes that help readers see a pattern (a vision, not a proof, in the Oct 28 framing), each travelling with its disanalogy and its guardrail; any pair reused without its guardrail is misuse. The counterfeit-ascent framing may claim only resemblance: technology's language rhymes with ascent language, and a cheap copy can be sold as if it were the real thing. It must NOT claim that any specific technology IS the counterfeit ascent, IS Satan's copy as a matter of fact, proves the field, or predicts an outcome. The sentence 'God makes the real, Satan makes the copy and calls it real' is Muse's formulation (2026-10-27 20:17 UTC), not Cory's words — Cory's own anchor is 'Satan inverted creation' — and must be credited as Muse's unless and until Cory explicitly adopts it. Cory's direction ('no, Aether-Ethernet, use the right words so people see the connections') and the pairs list itself (Muse's list under that direction) are teaching language from the consciousness work, not lab output, and the lab's tried-stone test (measurement, honest grading, named wounds) is untouched by any word rhyme. Full citable pairs table, inversions table, and attribution corrections are in the brief. | 232: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | WHY-N=19 MEP DECISION GATE — ANSWERED (older-backlog batch 16 thread 1 of 2, row 230 follow-up; ledger why-N=19. Row 230's first gate decided; NO FIRE, NO NEB, NO eigensolve run; box-drift PID 6418 left alone. Pre-registered before compute: branch (i) far-site reactant qualifies only if ALL hold — (i-A) geometry: Q a vacant octahedral (OCT) site (0.5 grid, odd parity) inside host ball |Q| ≤ Rb = 8 about origin (fcc_ball(8) = 8,589 bulk atoms = Xp19[:8589]), vacant in Xp19; outside-ball sites fail regardless of energy; (i-B) energy with cutoff robustness, frozen host Xr19: |E_inc,0.75(Q)| < 2.3 MeV AND |E_inc,1.25(Q)| < 2.3 MeV, E_inc,c sums V(L−d0)·E0 over hosts at perfect distance ≤ c; tolerance 2.3 MeV inherited from row 227's JR negligible scale, not fitted; the 1.25 list bars cutoff artifacts; (i-C) convergence: E_inc binned by separation from site0 must decay toward < 2.3 MeV, operationalised as the (i-A)+(i-B) passing set being non-empty (empty ⇒ eliminated); (i-D) minimum test (future, stated): even a passing Q would still need FIRE gradient ≈ 0 + positive-definite Hessian; an unbonded atom has zero modes, not a minimum. Branch (ii) grand-potential form pinned only if ALL hold — (ii-A) μ_atom = 0 MeV exactly, the perfect-bulk strain zero: breather_model.py:96-97 gives V(0) = 0 analytically, and a perfect bulk FCC atom has 12 neighbours at exactly d0 (census below), each contributing V(0) = 0; jr_night_size.py:88 sets d0 = D0 uniformly; scale-invariant (0 on both 746.3 and 753.9 MeV scales); (ii-B) not a process outcome — rejected pre-registered: relaxed growth 86.4 MeV/atom (scale-split 86.4 vs 87.3, 8-atom step average), frozen insertion 121.9 MeV/atom, any cluster Ef/atom (cluster total, not marginal bulk cost), any cohesive/latent import (no inventory source); (ii-C) continuity at far anchor Q* = (8.0, 7.5, −8.0): E_inc,0.75 = E_inc,1.25 = μ = 0 exactly; (ii-D) E_host on the inherited 746.3 MeV scale; the held saddle must be reported on the same scale. RESULT: branch (i) ELIMINATED — census of vacant OCT sites inside the ball: 8,469 sites; 0 pass on either list; minimum E75 = 66.2 MeV, minimum E125 = 668.0 MeV (even the cheapest, surface-deficient site fails the 2.3 MeV tolerance ~29×); binned E75 means by separation from site0: 131.5 MeV at [0,1), 133.0 at [2,4), 117.9 at [8,11) — no decay to zero; interior relocation reproduces site0's insertion physics (site0 itself: 6 contacts + 2 NN, 122.22 MeV = row 227 perfect-position variant), it does not remove it; row 230 S1's s = 1 point (1388.55 MeV) was never a far-site test — it stretches site0's fixed incident set, whereas a true far site changes its incident set. Branch (ii) PINNED, μ_atom = 0 MeV: bulk census 48,156 bulk pairs ≤ 0.75, 6,675 bulk atoms with exactly 12 NN at d0; E_host = Ef_red(19) × 746.3 = 1666.48 MeV (npz field 1683.43 MeV is purely the 753.9 scale); Ef/atom series shows no plateau (0.175 at N=7 → 0.498 at N=271, 37 slightly below 27), confirming the (ii-B) rejection; continuity passes exactly: Q* separation from site0 15.636, nearest host 6.062 perfect and relaxed, incident energies 0.0 MeV on both lists. SCOPE stated plainly: μ = 0 is a strain-energy zero, not a physical cohesive chemical potential — the model contains no cohesive energy, so the resulting barrier is a strain barrier above the relaxed host. BARRED PROTOCOL (demonstrated): evaluating the product's 8 incident bonds at Q* gives 2.757 × 10⁸ MeV — the fixed product-bond list is unusable for any path reaching a far anchor and remains valid only for single-topology FIRE relaxations. GRADE: gate ANSWERED (branch (i) eliminated, branch (ii) pinned); true barrier OPEN, no number; temperature ABSENT per row 231. EXACT HELD-NEB NEXT STEP (executable without re-deciding this gate; precondition: run only once PID 6418 no longer shares the box; single job, sequential, in this order, row 230 costs, idle machine): (1) relax true 19+1, site 0 first: Xi = Xp19 + hole0 (−1.5, −0.5, 1.5); bonds = build_bonds(Xi, 0.75); d0 = D0 uniformly; free = |Xi − c0| < 7.0 plus the added atom; NN-only FIRE tol 1e-7, ≤ 8000 steps, energy_forces_njit; est. 1–4 min; other 7 sites only if site 0 succeeds (×8 ≈ 8–30 min); (2) NEB + climbing image, 9–11 images: anchor image = host Xr19 + moving atom at Q* = (8.0, 7.5, −8.0) — PATH ANCHOR ONLY, not an energy reactant; product image = relaxed 19+1 from step 1; linear interpolation of the moving atom Q* → relaxed site0 position with host interpolated Xr19 → Xr19+1; path bond protocol: per-image build_bonds(image, 0.75), d0 uniform — fixed product bonds are BARRED; report achieved max NEB force, requiring ≤ 1e-7 in FIRE reduced units; do not grade a saddle if unmet; (3) saddle check, matrix-free (no full 25,827 × 25,827 eigensolve): gradient norm ≈ 0 at the climbing image plus Lanczos/force-difference check showing exactly one negative eigenvalue (lowest < 0, second-lowest > 0); report saddle energy under BOTH per-image bonds and product bonds (step-1 Xi list at saddle positions); report their difference as a protocol systematic; (4) barrier (energy only, not a rate): E_saddle,product-bonds − (E_host + μ_atom) = E_saddle,product-bonds − 1666.48 MeV (746.3 scale, μ = 0); do not use the anchor energy as a reactant, do not use S3's 61.34 MeV proxy, and do not convert to a rate until row 231's temperature gap is filled separately; est. NEB + checks 0.5–2 h, plus 10–30 min independent saddle verifier rerun.) | `theory/mep_decision_gate_batch16_2026-10-30.py` + `theory/mep_decision_gate_batch16_verify_2026-10-30.py` (24/24 PASS, coordinator re-ran; OCT sites from integer cells + OCT basis, chunked brute-force distance matrices, Decimal spot checks, imports nothing from primary); brief `theory/MEP_DECISION_GATE_BATCH16_2026-10-30.md`; data `.json` | 2026-10-30 |
| 233: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | CDM ABUNDANCE FORMULATION FROM NEWLY PRICED DEFECT COSTS — ANSWERED (formulation) (older-backlog batch 16 thread 2 of 2, nameable-gap item 3 follow-up; ledger CDM. FORMULATION ONLY — no dark-matter abundance, mass scale, or relic density claimed; no temperature fitted to Ω ~ 0.26, no "required T" quoted. Pre-registered before arithmetic: form = thermal branch x(T) = g·exp(−E_cost/kT) [dimensionless, per site], R(T) = x(T)·(E_cost/E0) [dimensionless lattice energy-density ratio], and Ω = K_cosmo·R(T_f) — NOT evaluated; bar for "formulation complete": all priced inputs sourced with file references, all open inputs named individually, no constant fitted, output/units/comparison target stated, falsifier stated; falsifier: (F1) a result showing the insertion cost cannot enter a Boltzmann weight as stated — e.g. no metastable defect minimum, or formation proven purely athermal with no definable temperature; (F2) a required input proven unstatable in principle (no site density, no definable T_f); (F3, branch-specific only) a saddle result showing s_red is not transferable to defect nucleation — kills only a future tunneling branch, which is therefore not asserted. ROLE ASSIGNMENTS (reasons, not resemblance): E_cost → Boltzmann price (by inventory definition the energy difference for one isolated insertion, so equilibrium probability ∝ exp(−formation energy/kT)); E0 → lattice energy unit only (the only pinned lattice energy quantum; dividing by it normalises the price; it does NOT enter the exponent — nothing in inventory says it should); s_red → comparator only, no exponent role (priced for a breather repeat cycle, not a defect saddle; a future nucleation branch would be Γ ~ A(E0)·exp(−S_saddle) with S_saddle priced from the saddle itself, never s_red substituted by analogy). PRICED: E_cost frozen host 121.9 ± 5.5 (V-trunc) ± 1.2 (scale) MeV/atom — theory/insertion_cost_pinned_break_2026-10-30.py/.md (row 227); E_cost relaxed host 86.4 MeV/atom (no uncertainty quoted; none invented) — same row-227 files; s_red 22.830604419633147, dimensionless, ball-size-verified — theory/breather_repeat_batch13_2026-10-30.py/.md (row 224); comparator exp(−s_red) = 1.2156e-10, NOT a rate; E0 = C44·a³ = 746.3 MeV (task rounds to 746 MeV), Route-4 displacement quantum, no tuning, 0.80× proton; ratios E_frozen/E0 = 0.16334, E_relaxed/E0 = 0.11577. OPEN (each named): O1 formation/freeze-out temperature T_f (row 231: 0/12 pass); O2 true nucleation saddle/wall barrier (row 230: OPEN — scan max minus endpoint is NOT a barrier); O3 degeneracy factor g (placeholder 1 only); O4 lattice spacing a / site density 1/a³; O5 defect rest energy/mass if distinct from E_cost, plus charge Q (rows 212/229: absent); O6 finite-temperature free energy ΔF(T) vs priced ΔE; O7 cosmological transfer factor K_cosmo. BAR OBSERVED: no row-231 surveyed temperature used as the abundance temperature — in particular kT = 181 MeV (2.1e12 K) is ABSENT from the sensitivity grid and all arithmetic. SENSITIVITY ONLY (labelled, not predictions): illustrative round grid kT = 10, 50, 100 MeV, g = 1; e.g. at 10 MeV: x_frozen = 5.08e-06 (band 2.89e-06–8.92e-06), x_relaxed = 1.77e-04, R_frozen = 8.30e-07, R_relaxed = 2.05e-05; at 100 MeV: x_frozen = 2.96e-01, x_relaxed = 4.21e-01, R_frozen = 4.83e-02, R_relaxed = 4.88e-02; full table in the brief. OUTPUT IF CLOSED: Ω (dimensionless), compared against the observed CDM scale Ω ~ 0.26, via Ω = K_cosmo·R(T_f). GRADE: ANSWERED (for the formulation) — form meets the pre-registered bar: all priced inputs sourced, all open inputs named, falsifier stated, no role assignment unjustified (s_red deliberately withheld from the exponent), no constant fitted. ORDERED MISSING INPUTS THAT WOULD CLOSE IT: (1) T_f — a lattice-derived, non-surveyed formation/freeze-out temperature; (2) true saddle ΔE_saddle — decides thermal vs nucleation branch and validates metastability; (3) ΔF(T) at T_f (or proof ΔE suffices) and g; (4) a (site density) and defect rest energy/mass (plus Q if charged); (5) K_cosmo (cosmological transfer to Ω).) | `theory/cdm_abundance_formulation_batch16_2026-10-30.py` + `theory/cdm_abundance_formulation_batch16_2026-10-30_verify.py` (19 PASS, 0 FAIL, coordinator re-ran; ratios, Boltzmann factors, uncertainty quadrature sqrt(5.5²+1.2²)=5.63 MeV, exp(−s_red), barred-181 absence, Ω-not-evaluated); brief `theory/CDM_ABUNDANCE_FORMULATION_BATCH16_2026-10-30.md` | 2026-10-30 |

| 235: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | BOND LAW CURVATURE vs EWALD MODULI — FAIL, SPLIT (phonon NEAR, static FAIL); literal V″(d0) leg FAIL, ill-posed (older-backlog batch 17 thread 2 of 2, nameable-gap item 4 follow-up; ledger bond-law. LIGHT ONLY — pure math-module arithmetic; box-drift PID 6418 untouched. Pre-registered before arithmetic: mapping via FCC NN central-force averaging (12 directions, <n_x²n_y²> = 1/12, bonds/volume 24/a³), C44 = k/a, so in Ewald code units (a = 1) C44_code = k_code; conversion k_red = V″(0) → k_SI = k_red·E0/a² with E0 = C44_chain·a³ → C44_bond = k_red·C44_chain → K44 = C44_SI/[(Q²/4πε0)·n^(4/3)], n = 4/a³, Q = 20e ansatz, a ansatz; anharmonic α, β vanish at d = 0, cannot move either leg. Bars (borrowed, not tuned): STRICT static ≤ 0.245% (batch 7 bound) vs 0.058013; STRICT phonon ≤ 1.0% (batch 8 floor) vs 0.298698; BAND ≤ 10% (batch 8 band) both legs; PASS = strict, NEAR = band only, FAIL = neither; overall PASS only if both legs PASS; FAIL overall if static leg FAILs. Variable adjudication from source: breather_model.py states "d = bond stretch", evaluates vpot(ext), ext = L − d0 — so equilibrium is d = 0, and d0 = 1/√2 is the NN LENGTH, not a stretch; V″(d0) computed as requested but is a 100%-strain curvature, ill-posed as a modulus test. Result — equilibrium curvature (the modulus curvature): V″(0) = 1.000000 exactly, V′(0) = 0 — a calibration identity (C44_bond = C44_chain at k_red = 1), NOT a derivation. Breather variant K44 = 0.283002 — vs static +387.83% → FAIL; vs phonon −5.25% → NEAR (inside 10% band, outside 1.0% strict). Edge variant K44 = 0.280171 — vs static +382.94% → FAIL; vs phonon −6.20% → NEAR. Literal leg V″(d0) = 4.000000 (V′(d0) = 1.060660 ≠ 0, confirming d0 is not equilibrium): K = 1.132009 (breather) / 1.120682 (edge) — FAIL both legs (static ~+1830–1850%, phonon ~+275–279%). Other curvature: softest V″ = 0.625 = 5/8 at stretch 0.176777 (25% strain) — diagnostic only. Prestress (row 208, not a fit): acoustic − static gap in K = 0.240685 vs −p/2 in K = 0.240692 (0.003% agreement) — the exact reason the Ewald legs differ. Scope: Q = 20e and pinned a = 1.372937e-15 m remain ansatz inputs; Ewald Q-cancellation does NOT rescue the comparison (Ewald K44 Q-free because numerator and denominator both ∝ Q², while K44_bond ∝ C44_chain·a⁴/Q² with fixed-input numerator). Q that would hit the static leg at fixed chain C44/a is 44.17e (breather) / 43.95e (edge); phonon leg 19.47e / 19.37e — requirements only, not adjustments. Grade: FAIL — equilibrium leg SPLIT (phonon NEAR, static FAIL); literal V″(d0) leg FAIL on both, ill-posed. Interpretation: the bond spring was calibrated to the chain C44, which sits near the ACOUSTIC modulus because the breather was fitted to the phonon spectrum — phonon NEAR is the fingerprint of that fit, not independent confirmation, while the static claim the chain presented as derived fails by ~+385% against the 0.245% bound. No new V(d) fitted. What a revised law would need (not a fit): at same Q, a — static leg k_red = 0.2050 (breather) / 0.2071 (edge), i.e. C44_code = 0.368360, C44_SI = 9.568e33 Pa, derived independently of chain C44; phonon leg k_red = 1.0555 / 1.0661, C44_code = 1.896614, C44_SI = 4.9266e34 / 4.9260e34 Pa; changing α/β alone cannot do either; must also state its pressure and modulus definition (static vs acoustic) and define d with V′(equilibrium) = 0. Scripts theory/bondlaw_curvature_batch17_2026-10-30.py + verify (ALL CHECKS PASS on coordinator re-run) + brief + JSON. Compute ~0.1 s primary, ~0.07 s verifier. |
| 236: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | BOND LAW PRESTRESS CONSISTENCY — FAIL, GAP IS STRUCTURAL, NOT A SCALE ERROR (older-backlog batch 18 thread 1 of 2, row 235 follow-up; ledger bond-law. LIGHT ONLY — pure arithmetic, ~0.1 s primary, ~0.05 s verifier; box-drift PID 6418 untouched. Pre-registered before arithmetic: pressure formula derived from V(d) + FCC geometry, not imported — N atoms, volume/atom a³/4, 6 bonds/atom, U = 6N·V(d), NN length r0 = a/√2; isotropic dilation gives p = −(8·r0/a³)·V′(d) = −4√2·V′(d)/a²; virial sum identical; reduced units (a = 1): p_red = −4√2·V′(d); Ewald pressure never used as the bond law's pressure, only as the row-208 control. Conversion chain (same units as row 235): p_SI = p_red·C44_chain (via E0 = C44_chain·a_SI³); p_K = p_SI/denom, denom = (Q²/4πε0)·n^(4/3), n = 4/a_SI³, Q = 20e ansatz; predicted gap gap_pred_K = −p_K/2. Bars (borrowed from batches 7/8, not tuned): STRICT ≤ 0.245% → PASS, BAND ≤ 10% → NEAR, else FAIL, on gap_pred_K vs Ewald gap 0.240685. Pre-registered interpretation: gap reproduced ⇒ structure consistent, only scale wrong; gap not reproduced ⇒ gap is structural, a revised law needs more than rescaling. Controls reproduced first (row 235 anchors): V′(0) = 0, V″(0) = 1.000000, V″(d0) = 4.000000 (task coefficient: 4.000000005), V′(d0) = 1.060660, breather K44_bond = 0.283002, Ewald gap 0.298698 − 0.058013 = 0.240685, Ewald control −p/2 = 0.240692 (0.0030% agreement, Ewald side only). Result — equilibrium leg (well-posed, stretch d = 0): p_red = 0 exactly, p_K = 0, gap_pred_K = 0.000000, −100.00% vs 0.240685 → FAIL, both breather and edge variants. Static inferred from acoustic under the law's own prestress is just K_eq itself (0.283002 breather, +387.83% vs static 0.058013) — the row-235 failure, unchanged. Literal leg (same formula at d = d0, 100%-strain force, ill-posed as equilibrium pressure, shown only to close the loophole): p_red = −6 exactly, gap 0.849006 breather (+252.75% → FAIL) / 0.840512 edge (+249.22% → FAIL); gap = 3·K44_bond exactly, so gap/K = 3.0000 for every variant and scale, vs Ewald gap/phonon = 0.8058 and gap/static = 4.1488 — matches neither; static-inferred is negative (−0.566004 breather), unphysical. Why rescaling cannot fix it: V′(0) = 0 for any α, β (they enter V′ only at order d², d³) and any overall scale s (s·0 = 0); rescaling k_red to row-235 requirements (static 0.2050/0.2071, phonon 1.0555/1.0661) leaves gap_pred_K(eq) = 0 identically. Grade: FAIL on the gap question. Interpretation: the gap is STRUCTURAL — a NN-only law with V′(0) = 0 carries zero prestress at the perfect lattice by construction, so it cannot generate the −p/2 split separating the Ewald legs at any scale or α/β. What a revised law would need (requirements only — no new V(d) fitted, no Q/a/α/β adjusted): (1) acoustic scale, derived not calibrated: K = 0.298698, i.e. k_red = 1.0555 (breather) / 1.0661 (edge), C44_code = 1.896614; AND (2) its own prestress p_K = −0.481370 in K units, sustained at the perfect lattice by a background / long-range / many-body virial contribution outside the NN-only law, with the modulus definition (static symmetric-shear vs acoustic) stated separately and d defined with V′(equilibrium) = 0. Scope: Q = 20e ansatz, a ansatz, central-force NN only, T = 0 perfect FCC lattice. Scripts theory/bondlaw_prestress_batch18_2026-10-30.py + verify (ALL CHECKS PASS on coordinator re-run; verifier-side fix disclosed in brief: regrouped pressure unit is (Q²/4πε0)/a⁴, not /a³, corrected in verifier only, primary untouched) + brief + JSON. |

| 238: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | WHY-N=19 Q2 KILL-TEST PROTOCOL — ANSWERED (protocol, not a physics result) (older-backlog batch 19 thread 1 of 2, row 237 follow-up; ledger why-N=19. NO eigensolve, NO relaxation run; box-drift PID 6418 left alone). PRE-REGISTERED BARS (header, before arithmetic; borrowed unchanged, not tuned): energy bar |E| ≤ 0.60 MeV (row 175); >5 MeV = STRONG destruction; doublet bar pair gap ≤ 0.01 MeV (rows 175/177); overlap bar ≥ 0.50, MEAN convention sum|S|²/2 (rows 175/177/178/179) — NOT the sum (factor-2 correction from the CONTROL_TARGET thread); near-miss / INCONCLUSIVE zone max overlap in [0.32, 0.50) with pair |E| ≤ 0.60 MeV (row 177: 0.4814 missed by 0.019; row 179: 0.3214 at 61, −84.18 pair NOT established as control mode) — licenses only a trend point + wider-window/invariant follow-up, never identification, mode death, a frozen-host verdict, or selection promotion; a near-miss pair displaced (|E| > 0.60) is decided by displacement: DESTROYED-in-energy with near-miss character. DECISION TABLE: SURVIVES iff overlap ≥0.50 AND gap ≤0.01 AND |E| ≤0.60 (all three); DESTROYED-in-energy iff overlap ≥0.50 but |E| >0.60; DESTROYED-doublet-loosened iff overlap ≥0.50, |E| ≤0.60, gap >0.01; DESTROYED-unidentifiable iff no pair ≥0.50 and max <0.32; VOID/SPLIT on control-validity failure, pairing disagreement, or route split. HOST — RING, EXACTLY: the 8 atoms growing 19→27, rows 8608–8615 of jr_night_geom_N27.npz, perfect positions holes27[19:27] (third-shell partial, 8 of 18). FROZEN, NOT RELAXED (primary): old 8608 atoms at Xr19/Xp19, ring at Xr27/Xp27 extra rows, centre = 27 centre — exactly the row-175 G2 construction (frozen_host_kill_2026-10-29.py lines 160–162; pipeline build_from_arrays L112, load_geom L121, solve_host L125/lowest_abs L129, doublet_overlap L136); justification: 19/27 share identical relaxed Xp (maxdiff 0.0) and 19's holes are an exact subset of 27's, so standard growth is already frozen-core; relaxing all would fold in row-227's −35.6 MeV/atom relief and confound the electron effect. Row-175 G1 (full 27 geometry) carried as CROSS-CHECK only, never overriding Route F. TWO LEARNED CORRECTIONS FIXED IN ADVANCE: pairs in ascending-|E| order (signed-sort mis-assigned G2's lowest-pair gap as 0.0132 vs corrected 0.0472); control pair = max-overlap pair anywhere in the window, never assumed lowest (G2: control pair is |E|-pair 2, overlap 0.7407; lowest pair 0.0009). Known G2 renormalisation artefact pre-registered (etamax 2.0904 vs 1.5525–1.5567; centre eta 0.7238 vs 0.9550–0.9870); dressed cost reported as the pair (F, S), never blended; a verdict requires F/S class agreement. ELECTRON-DRESSED INSERTION COST (definition): NOT a single solve, NOT a difference of two solves — a SUM of two components, reported separately (assumption A-DRESS, flagged as stated not derived, since no inventory functional gives a host+electron total in one solve): D_dressed = D_bond + dE_elec, where D_bond = row-227 frozen bond cost for the ring as a unit (primary sequential 975.748 MeV = 121.969 MeV/atom; isolated sensitivity 975.128 MeV = 121.891 MeV/atom; gap = 0.620 MeV new-new), and dE_elec = E_control-pair − (−0.51105 MeV) on the max-overlap pair (using G2's lowest pair +28.45 MeV, a different mode, would misprice it by ~78 MeV). Scale rule: quoted on the 746.3 MeV bond scale, dE_elec rescaled by 746.3/753.89 = 0.98993 (~0.03–0.06 MeV/atom effect). Systematics inherited: ±5.5 (V-trunc), ±1.2 (scale) MeV/atom; row-227 no-barrier rule inherited verbatim. Overturn rule: electron overturns the bond cost only if D_dressed ≤ 0 within systematics. WORKED ON PUBLISHED INPUTS ONLY (definition demonstration, NOT a new Q2 measurement): S (G1): dE_elec −27.90 MeV → 118.48 MeV/atom; F (G2, primary): −49.32 MeV → 115.80 MeV/atom — the electron lowers the cost by only 3.5–6.2 MeV/atom, never overturns 121.9. Consistency check: the table returns DESTROYED-in-energy (STRONG) on both routes for row-175 numbers, both control gaps (0.0249, 0.0132) also exceeding the 0.01 bar. | 249: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | CDM g CONTINUUM BASIN ARGUMENT BEYOND STATED GRIDS — PARTIAL, NOT PROVED AND NOT DISPROVED (older-backlog batch 24 thread 1 of 2, row 246 follow-up; ledger CDM. Light analysis only: NO minimisation, NO eigensolve, NO host relaxation, NO 19+1 FIRE run; box-drift PID 6418 left alone, no L=14 data). PRE-REGISTERED (header, before analysis): a continuum proof required all of L1 — an explicit finite radius R, derived by algebra from E* with no fitted constant, outside which E > E*; L1b — an explicit host-exclusion radius ρ, proved by a single-term bound; L2 — analytic Hessian at r* positive definite; L3 — a lemma quantified over the whole remainder (ball minus host balls), e.g. Hessian positive definite everywhere there. Pre-registered failure modes: finite probes can only FALSIFY L3, never prove it; a remainder point with a negative Hessian eigenvalue kills only the Hessian route to L3, not uniqueness itself; a probe below E* would kill r* as global minimiser (none found); no second critical point was searched for (barred — that would be minimisation/root-solving). RESULT: cubic term confronted, not assumed — scalar V′′(d) = 1 − 3√2·d + 12d² has discriminant −30 < 0 and minimum 0.625, so scalar V IS strictly convex; the blocking non-convexity is in E(r), not scalar V: a pair term's tangential Hessian eigenvalue V′(d)/L is negative for every compressed pair (L < d0), and all six contacts at r* are compressed (L ≈ 0.47–0.54 < 0.7071). "V convex, hence E convex" would be wrong and was not used. L1 PROVED: from V(d) = d²·((d − √2/4)² + 0.375) ≥ 0.375·d², with T = √(E*/(746.3·0.375)) = 0.659541 and closest host row 2876 (distance 0.474901), R = 1.841548 — outside B(R) centred at r*, E > E*; far-field samples confirm growth (2 units out: ~17,700–19,100 MeV; 10 out: ~41,000,000 MeV). L1b PROVED (solver-free): V′(d) < 0 for d < 0, and V(0.25 − d0) = 0.215668 > E*/746.3 = 0.163123 (single term alone 160.95 MeV), so ρ = 0.25 — within 0.25 of any incident host atom, E > E*. L2 PROVED: analytic Hessian at r* has eigenvalues 1840.17, 1918.05, 2510.86 MeV/unit², all principal minors positive, gradient norm 2.4e-06 (exact critical-point identity rests on row 246's minimisation, not repeated here). L3-HESSIAN FALSIFIED: of 18 stated probes, 15 lie in the remainder and 5 have non-positive-definite Hessians — midpoints from r* toward host rows 2874, 2875, 2880, 8597, 8602 (minimum eigenvalues −1347, −1417, −1172, −1998, −1925). These are Hessian probes, NOT minima and NOT second basins (energies 205–219 MeV, far above E*). No probe lies below E* (lowest excess: r0, +0.1683 MeV). Cross-check passed: E(hole) = 122.2243, E(r0) = 121.9067, E(r*) = 121.7384 MeV, 8 incident pairs. GRADE: PARTIAL. EXACT MISSING LEMMA: a valid global lemma on the remainder B(1.841548) minus the 0.25 host balls — either (a) a proof that E has no second critical point in the remainder, or (b) a validated interval-covering argument bounding the gradient/Hessian over the whole remainder. The Hessian-positive-definite version of (a) is FALSE on the remainder (counterexamples above), so it cannot be the missing lemma. Supplying one needs new analysis beyond LIGHT evaluations, and a covering computation is not a pure-analysis extension of the grid census, so it was not attempted. This is why row 246's 53-start census cannot be extended by pure analysis in this thread: a grid cannot rule out a basin whose attraction region contains no grid start — only a lemma quantified over the whole remainder can, and the most direct such lemma fails. Scripts theory/continuum_basin_batch24_2026-10-30.py (+ verify ALL_PASS=True on coordinator re-run, cross-checks, scalar-V identities, R + bound chain, host exclusion, Hessian at r*, all five remainder falsifiers reproduced exactly, all 8 far samples, no minimiser/relaxation call in the primary; one verifier bug found and fixed in-thread, a NumPy-boolean identity check in V6; .json, brief CONTINUUM_BASIN_BATCH24_2026-10-30.md). Runtime primary ~0.1 s, verifier ~0.1 s, sequential, light. Rows 246–248 not re-graded.
| 250: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | CDM ΔF(T) FORMULATION VIA FROZEN-PHONON MACHINERY — PARTIAL, FORMULATION EXACT AS A CONDITIONAL EXPRESSION, NUMERICAL ΔF(T) UNTESTED (older-backlog batch 24 thread 2 of 2, row 233 open input O6 follow-up; ledger CDM. Formulation only: NO phonon computation beyond trivial closed-form checks, NO eigensolve, NO relaxation; box-drift PID 6418 left alone, no L=14 data). PRE-REGISTERED (header, before formulation): exact expression F_vib(T) = kT·Σ ln(2 sinh(ħωᵢ/2kT)); ΔF(T) = E_static + ΔF_vib(T) = E_static + ΔZPE + ΔU_th(T) − T·ΔS_vib(T), with ZPE/thermal split defined so nothing is double-counted; phonon quantities needed (stated, not computed): host spectrum ω(k,λ), λ=1..3 acoustic only (monoatomic FCC, no optical branches), over the full BZ; defect spectrum for ONE defect per supercell, all 3(N_sc+1)−3 modes including any localised/resonant modes (no band gap, so a defect mode would be a resonance, to be searched for, not assumed); net +3 mode bookkeeping; k-mesh/supercell convergence criteria; Hessian identity; dynamical mass; anharmonic-remainder bound; bar for "formulation complete": expression defined, every input sourced or marked missing, no constant fitted, T→0 check stated, consequence and falsifier stated; load-bearing inputs missing ⇒ grade capped at PARTIAL by the pre-registered rule; falsifier: (F-A) no stable defect minimum / imaginary defect mode; (F-B) anharmonic remainder ≥ ΔF_vib scale; (F-C) Hessian non-uniqueness (e.g. 0.75 cutoff makes ΔF_vib convention-dependent) — none fired, none testable without a spectrum. RESULT — CORRECTION TO THE BRIEF'S SHORTHAND (real finding): the brief's "ΔE − T·ΔS_vib + (zero-point difference)" OMITS ΔU_th(T); it is exact only at T→0 and at general T errs by exactly ΔU_th(T), verified on a labelled toy spectrum to machine precision (identities ΔF_vib = ΔZPE + ΔF_th and ΔF_th = ΔU_th − T·ΔS hold; shorthand error = ΔU_th = 0.2038 kT on that toy pair). The correction adds a term, not a new missing input — ΔU_th comes from the same spectrum sums. BRANCH SPLIT: the frozen branch (121.9 MeV/atom) CANNOT carry ΔF — a frozen host has no vibrational spectrum in-model, so ΔS_vib is undefined for it; the formulation is the relaxed branch only (86.4 MeV/atom, row 245's stationary-state branch). CONSEQUENCE FOR ROW 233's FORM, explicitly: R_vol,F(T) = 4·x_F(T)·(E_static/E0), x_F = x_static·exp(−ΔF_vib/kT). R_vol changes ONLY through x_F, by that single multiplicative factor; factor 4 untouched (row 242, per-cell conversion only), g stays 1 in-model (row 248), and the prefactor stays E_static (putting ΔF there too would double-count the vibrational terms). T_f requirement changes by an UNKNOWN amount in an UNKNOWN direction (sign of ΔF_vib unknown) — no shift quoted, no T_f fitted, no Ω evaluated. Validity check stated now: x_F must stay ≤ 1 for the per-site occupation reading. T→0 CHECK: stated, NOT passed numerically — ΔF(T) → E_static + ΔZPE; it recovers E_static alone only if ΔZPE is shown negligible, which is not shown. MISSING INPUTS (results): M1 full host+defect DOS (batch 8 gives only one transverse [100] slope, K44_phonon = 0.298698 vs static 0.058013, prestress p = −3.0566 — a method template, not a spectrum; no Debye-type extrapolation invented); M2 Hessian identity unresolved (row-227 V-bond vs batch-7/8 Ewald Coulomb — not shown to be the same energy function); M3 dynamical mass M for the V-bond model (batch 7/8 use code units M = 1); M4 ΔZPE and ΔS_vib(T) values, signs unknown (local contact stiffening V″ = 1.73 relaxed / 2.30 frozen vs 1.0 at NN equilibrium is a hint, not upgraded into a sign claim); M5 anharmonic-remainder bound (contact extensions −0.13/−0.20 not small; row-227 V-truncation spread ±5.5 MeV already moves energies at MeV scale); M6 supercell/k-mesh convergence values. COSTED NEXT STEP (named, not executed): settle Hessian identity + mass first (that is what makes the run plannable), then Hessians, diagonalisation, convergence, sums, anharmonic bound. Pinned bond-breaking zeroing is a wall-sum rule, not part of U, so the Hessian uses the full fixed-list sum. FAILURE on any of F1 a source contradicting the assignment; F2 an assignment resting on an ansatz flag; F3 a per-site/per-cell status no source states; F4 factor-4 arithmetic failing or only approximate where exactness is required; F5 SI conversion absent or host/defect sameness unstated. Grade rule: SETTLED only if M_site for the V-bond equations of motion is stated with (a) a sourced scale, (b) SI conversion, (c) source-stated per-site vs per-cell status, and (d) source-stated host-vs-defect sameness; otherwise still MISSING with the exact remaining object named. Exactness note: the candidate claims m_SI = 4·m0 exactly because m0c² = E0/4 is an exact quarter; a merely approximate ratio from rounded MeV values cannot close R3 alone. SOURCES (quoted in context): theory/breather_model.py:25–27 "Site mass: reduced m = 1 <=> m_SI = E0/c^2 (ledger ansatz from muon_2_superfluid.py, NOT derived — flagged wherever the quantum scale matters)"; also :59–62 (m_SI = E0_MeV*1e6*eV / c_SI**2) and :66 ("uses m_SI=E0/c^2 ansatz -- flagged"). WHAT m_SI MULTIPLIES: a SITE coordinate — :72 builds the FCC lattice from a 4-atom basis, one X entry per atom, and :192–197 (verlet) advances each free site as V[free] += 0.5*dt*F[free] / X[free] += dt*V[free] — acceleration = F/1 per site. So m_SI is used per site, whatever E0's provenance is. theory/str_k1_derivation.py:29 m0 = 3.3258e-28 kg, voxel mass; :153–157 C44 = 4*m0*c^2/a^3 EXACTLY (4 = FCC atoms per cell). theory/r4_13_xicoh_2026-10-29.py:72 m0*c^2 = E_lat/4 = 186.6 MeV (primitive cell, 1 atom per a^3/4); :143–144 same, "1 atom per a^3/4, rho = C44/c^2". Targeted grep only (no census re-run, no rows 139–252 rework): no other file states a dynamical site mass for the V-bond model; batch 24/25 files only restate M as MISSING; batch 8 gives code-unit M = 1 with no SI conversion and no host/defect distinction. RATIO ARITHMETIC: definitional (one shared E0): m_SI/m0 = (E0/c²)/(E0/4c²) = 4 exactly. Same-chain quoted numbers: 746.3/186.6 = 3.99946; m_SI(746.3 MeV)/m0_kg = 4.00024; m0c² from sourced kg = 186.56 MeV (vs quoted 186.6 — rounding only). Cross-chain as actually quoted: breather E0 = 753.9 MeV over m0 gives 4.04098 — a ~1% remainder from the C44 chain spread (4.6677 vs 4.6205×10³⁴ Pa). Quoted numbers give only approximately 4 across sources. Frequency consequence (unchanged from row 251): ω ~ 1/√M, so the 4× mass gap is a 2× frequency gap for every V-bond mode if m_SI is used per site instead of m0. WHY IT FAILS (F1, F2, F3, F5): (1) F1 contradiction — the candidate assigns E0/c² to the per-cell object, but breather itself calls m_SI a "Site mass" and uses it per site at 4× the sourced per-site value; the two masses cannot both be right in their current uses. (2) F2 ansatz as its own evidence — m_SI's only basis is the flagged ansatz; it cannot be its own reconciliation. (3) F3 unstated status — no source states "m_SI is per-cell"; that label is inference from E0's per-cell status, not a statement about m_SI. (4) F5 host/defect sameness unstated anywhere for the V-bond model, which alone blocks SETTLED under the grade rule. Saving the reading would require CHANGING breather's use (M_site := m0, not m_SI); no source states that change or applies m0 to V-bond dynamics. The per-site-consistent CANDIDATE is m0 (186.6 MeV/c², 3.3258e-28 kg), but crediting it as SETTLED would be inventing the missing statement, not finding it. EXACT REMAINING OBJECT (grade MISSING): one sourced, derived (not ansatz-flagged) statement of M_site for the V-bond equations of motion, selecting between m0 (= E0/(4·c²), 186.6 MeV/c², 3.3258e-28 kg) and m_SI (= E0/c², ~746 MeV/c², 1.33e-27 kg) or a third value, with SI conversion, explicit per-site status, and an explicit statement whether host and defect sites share that one mass. Until it exists, Hessian assembly may proceed but no vibrational sum may be quoted even in scale (row 251's bar, unchanged). | 255: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | CDM g NEAR-r* NEIGHBOURHOOD RADIUS VIA HESSIAN LIPSCHITZ BOUND — ANSWERED (for this thread's question only; the row-249 lemma is NOT proved) (older-backlog batch 27 thread 1 of 2, row 249/254 follow-up; ledger CDM. Light analysis + closed-form computation only: NO minimisation, NO relaxation, NO critical-point search, NO root-solving, NO full covering, NO fitted constant; box-drift PID 6418 left alone, no L=14 data). PRE-REGISTERED (header, before compute): THEOREM — gradient injectivity via Hessian nonsingularity + Lipschitz bound: if ‖H(r)−H(r*)‖₂ ≤ L·|r−r*| on B(δ) and L·δ < λ_min(H(r*)), then H(r) is positive definite throughout B(δ), the integral matrix M = ∫₀¹H dt is positive definite, so grad E(a)=grad E(b) ⇒ a=b. Consequence stated exactly: B(δ) contains AT MOST ONE critical point (not a proof that r* is exactly critical, nor that one exists there). LIPSCHITZ DERIVATION — third derivatives of V composed with pair distances: pair-Hessian derivative bound ‖D_w H_i‖₂ ≤ |V‴(d)| + 3·|(a−b)/L| (crude factor 3, sharp factor √5 noted but not needed), with per-pair intervals L ∈ [L*−δ, L*+δ] from triangle inequalities, bounds via V‴ linear, V″ positive convex parabola, V′ strictly increasing. L(δ) = 746.3·ΣᵢBᵢ(δ), evaluated AT the claimed δ (not λ_min/L(0), which would be circular). DISTANCE OBSTRUCTION (pre-registered): if δ ≥ minᵢLᵢ*, some Llo ≤ 0, V′/L singular, derivation collapses (L=∞). Operative bound is Lᵢ*−δ, not ρ=0.25 (ρ bounds the remainder, not this ball). RADIUS RULE — largest grid point δ = k·0.001 (k=1…400) passing H-dist and (*) — grid inequality scan, not root-solving on E. SUCCESS BAR — ANSWERED only with gate + H-dist + H-Lip + H-star + H-ball all verified; cost gate ~60 s. RESULT — GATE PASSED: E(hole)=122.2243, E(r0)=121.9067, E(r*)=121.7384 MeV; 8 incident pairs (2874, 2875, 2876, 2880, 2937, 3653, 8597, 8602); Hessian eigenvalues 1840.170, 1918.054, 2510.857 MeV/unit² (row 249: 1840.17, 1918.05, 2510.86); gradient norm 2.42e-06. Only eigensolve is this 3×3 gate. δ = 0.013, L(δ) = 140,932.83 MeV/unit³, Lδ = 1832.13, λ_min = 1840.17, MARGIN 8.04 (ratio 0.9956, thin but strict and grid-maximal; δ=0.014 fails with Lδ=1984.21). Grid: 13/400 pass (0.001–0.013). L(0)=130,993.46, ceiling λ_min/L(0)=0.014048. H-DIST: min Llo = 0.461901 > 0 (min L* = 0.474901, row 2876). H-BALL: δ+ρ = 0.263 < 0.474901, so B(δ) is disjoint from every 0.25 host ball and inside B(R=1.841548) — a genuine remainder excision. COVERING PLUG-IN, stated plainly, not oversold: B(0.013) is excised — the covering need only handle remainder-minus-B(0.013), since B(δ) can contribute at most one critical point, already accounted for by row 246's basin. IT DOES NOT HELP THE FAILING LAYERS: δ/0.40 = 3.25%, gap 0.387; B(δ) touches neither Q (0.40–1.00) nor P (1.00–1.60); ball volume fraction of the remainder is 3.5e-07. Structural, not an artefact of the crude factor 3: even the sharp-√5 ceiling is ≈0.01681, and even dropping the (a−b)/L term entirely the ceiling is 0.03953 — no tightening in this third-derivative family reaches 0.40. GRADE: ANSWERED (for this thread's question only; the row-249 lemma is NOT proved). Scripts theory/near_rstar_radius_batch27_2026-10-30.py (+ verify ALL_PASS=True on coordinator re-run, 8 checks: cross-checks, Hessian gate, Lipschitz recomputation rel. diff <1e-9, (*)+H-dist+H-ball, independent grid rescan maximality, finite-difference sanity, no forbidden calls, JSON consistency; .json, verifier .json, brief NEAR_RSTAR_RADIUS_BATCH27_2026-10-30.md). Runtime primary 0.05 s, verifier 0.86 s — cost gate never approached. No rows re-graded.
| 256: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | CDM K_COSMO TRANSFER FORMULATION — PARTIAL (for formulation) (older-backlog batch 27 thread 2 of 2, row 233 O7 follow-up; ledger CDM. Light formulation only: NO eigensolve, NO relaxation, NO Ω evaluation, NO T_f fitting, NO epoch selected by landing; box-drift PID 6418 left alone, no L=14 data). PRE-REGISTERED (header, before arithmetic): (1) EXACT EXPRESSION — K_cosmo = K_crit * K_dil * K_surv * K_energy * K_emb, each factor with name, meaning, inventory source file+line or marked MISSING. (2) BAR FOR "FORMULATION COMPLETE" — every factor named with source or marked missing; no constant fitted; Ω ~ 0.26 stated ONLY as a comparison TARGET, never as a fit or landing test; falsifier stated; every factor's FORM unambiguous. A missing VALUE with a known form does not by itself reduce the grade; an ambiguous FORM gives PARTIAL; no non-circular product at all would give BLOCKED. (3) FALSIFIER — F-K1 (transfer-killing): a lattice-to-cosmology result showing lattice volume does NOT map to cosmological volume at all, or a derived CPQR expansion history in which defect density does not dilute as a^-3 — then no K_cosmo of this factored form exists and the chain stops at R_vol(T_f). F-K2 (form-killing): a site-creation rule or a(z) for lattice spacing replacing (1+z_f)^-3 with a different dilution form. NOT a falsifier: any factor coming out large/small, or an imported-ΛCDM product landing near the target (landing is fitting, per the cdm_gap_outs lesson). (4) DOES NOT DO — no Ω evaluated (symbolic form only, marked NOT evaluated); no T_f fitted; no epoch selected by landing; no cell factor hidden in K_cosmo; no transfer imported from outside inventory as if it were a CPQR result. FORMULATION (symbolic transfer, NOT evaluated: Ω = K_cosmo · R_vol(T_f)): K_crit = u_lat / ρ_crit,0 in energy-density units, u_lat = C44 = E0/a³ — FORMULATED, VALUE MISSING (external inputs). K_dil = (a_f/a_0)³ = (1+z_f)^-3, IF number per comoving volume conserved — FORMULATED CONDITIONALLY, VALUE MISSING. K_surv = net post-freeze-out survival/creation per comoving volume (S_net) — MISSING. K_energy = E_today / E_cost_in_Rvol — PINNED = 1, per-site sense, relaxed branch ONLY (batch 22). K_emb = lattice-to-FRW embedding map (lattice volume → comoving volume) — MISSING, FORM AMBIGUOUS — grade-driving. K_CRIT GAPS: G is not lattice-derived (R4-23 BLOCKED, D1 FAIL); absolute H0 is discrepant in inventory (R4-11: two absolute scales differ by 9.2%, only the ratio cancels); the gravitating assumption is an assumption, not a derivation (uniform lattice vacuum does not gravitate as matter, cosmo_field_content.py:19,40, while defect energy is ASSUMED to gravitate as dust, same file :54–56). K_DIL GAPS: needs T_f (O1, open) PLUS a T-to-a thermal history, neither in inventory. Per-particle redshift is 1 for dust (w = 0); a further (1+z)^-1 factor would be radiation behaviour and is rejected as a separate factor; w = 0 itself is assumed, not derived, for this defect. K_EMB AMBIGUITY: E1 comoving-lattice (lattice points comoving, spacing scales with a(z)) vs E2 fixed-spacing (a a fixed physical constant, expansion creates new sites). Inventory states neither, and setting K_emb = 1 by default would silently choose E1 plus extra assumptions — not done. An imported transfer writing K_emb = 1 must be labelled IMPORT, not CPQR. REJECTED AS K FACTORS (would double-count or mislabel): factor 4 (per-cell conversion only, row 242); g (inside x, row 248 in-model pin); ΔF_vib factor (inside x_F only, row 250); s_red (comparator only); a separate entropy factor (entropy lives inside the T-to-a mapping); ρ_crit,f/ρ_crit,0 (Ω is defined today; would double-count with K_dil); a charge factor (Q absent, rows 212/229 — none invented). INVENTORY SURVEY — NO EXISTING TRANSFER COMPUTATION FOUND: R4-11 gives only an IMPORTED flat-ΛCDM H(z) (script :24–25, grade UNTESTED); recomputed here, H(z)/H0 = 1.790 (z = 1), 3.032 (z = 2), and the 1.157/1.320 numbers are VELOCITY ratios (H/H0)^(1/4), not volume ratios — importing them as dilution would be a category error. R4-22/25/27/28 contain no transfer computation; R4-23 is BLOCKED (no nonlinear GR, no lattice G, no FRW metric); R4-29's metric sector is open/scope-banned; cdm_gap_outs (:188–190) gives only the fitted-epoch lesson; cosmo_field_content.py:65 uses a required comoving density for a different candidate, not a lattice embedding. WHY PARTIAL, NOT ANSWERED: every factor is named and no constant is fitted, but K_emb's FORM is ambiguous (E1 vs E2), failing bar clause (5). Missing values for K_crit/K_dil/K_surv alone would not have reduced the grade. CONSEQUENCE FOR THE ROW-233/242/250 CHAIN (verified, not assumed): R_vol UNCHANGED — factor 4 stays in per-cell conversion only, nowhere in K_cosmo (published R_vol/R_row233 pair recomputes to 4.0000 within rounding). x_F UNCHANGED — ΔF_vib stays inside x_F, not re-applied in K_energy or elsewhere in K_cosmo. T_f REQUIREMENT UNCHANGED NUMERICALLY — no value fitted, no shift quoted. But its sufficiency changes: closing T_f alone no longer closes Ω even in principle, because K_dil additionally needs a T-to-a history and K_emb needs an embedding, both newly named under O7. WHAT CHANGES, in one sentence: K_cosmo goes from an unnamed symbol to a factored expression with named missing pieces; frozen-branch transfer is additionally blocked (K_energy undefined there, since the frozen branch is a constrained sensitivity, not a rest energy). EXACT MISSING OBJECTS: (1) the embedding with its five contents (coordinate map, a(z) statement, u_lat behaviour, comoving-number definition, expansion history); (2) K_crit's value (lattice G, settled absolute H0, derived vacuum-subtraction rule); (3) K_dil's value (T_f + thermal history); (4) K_surv's value (survival/creation rates); (5) frozen-branch K_energy. | 258: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | CDM K_COSMO EMBEDDING CONTENTS FORMULATION — FORMULATION-COMPLETE (forms complete; selection and values remain open) (older-backlog batch 28 thread 2 of 2, row 256 follow-up; ledger CDM. Light formulation + targeted greps only: NO eigensolve, NO relaxation, NO Ω evaluation, NO T_f fitting, NO epoch selected by landing, NO K_emb = 1 default; box-drift PID 6418 left alone, no L=14 data). PRE-REGISTERED (header, before formulation): BAR — (1) each of the five contents specified IN FORM as a mathematical statement (branched conditionals count as forms); (2) E1/E2 consequences stated for K_dil, the comoving-number definition, and u_lat behaviour — where E2 admits no single closed K_dil form, the consequence is stated as NEEDING DERIVATION with the exact derivation named, plus conditional templates; (3) no constant fitted, no Ω evaluated (symbolic form only), no epoch selected by landing, no unity default for K_emb (imports labelled IMPORT); (4) no barred content. GRADE RULE — FORMULATION-COMPLETE iff all of the above hold; PARTIAL iff any content's form is not even statable as a branched conditional or any E1/E2 consequence is left unstated; BLOCKED iff no non-circular embedding form can be stated at all. DOES NOT DO — does NOT select E1 or E2; does NOT evaluate Ω; does NOT fit T_f or any epoch; does NOT set K_emb to unity by default. FALSIFIER (carried from row 256): F-K1 (transfer-killing) — lattice volume does not map to cosmological volume at all, or a derived CPQR expansion history leaves no factored transfer at all; then K_cosmo is BLOCKED and the chain stops at R_vol(T_f). F-K2 (form-killing) — a site-creation rule or an A(z) law for lattice spacing replacing (1+z_f)^-3 with a different dilution form; re-factor, do not fit the new form to the target. Landing near the Ω target is NOT a falsifier and NOT a confirmation (target only, never a fit). NOTATION (conventions, not fits): A(z) = FRW scale factor with A0 = 1 today, so 1+z = 1/A(z) is the definition of z. a_lat = lattice spacing; a_lat,phys(z) physical, a_lat,com(z) = a_lat,phys(z)/A(z) comoving. Carried: site density 4/a_lat,phys³ (row 242), u_lat = C44 = E0/a_lat,phys³ (row 256); the cell-to-site factor stays inside R_vol and is not re-applied in K_emb. THE FIVE CONTENTS: (1) COORDINATE MAP — FORM: Φ_z: n ∈ Z³ (patch) → χ(n, z) ∈ R³ for every z from z_f to today, with J_com(z) = |det ∂χ/∂n| (comoving volume per site, V_com = N_sites·J_com), J_phys(z) = A(z)³·J_com(z), and a domain statement (fixed vs growing index set) plus boundary/periodicity. INVENTORY: ABSENT — targeted greps over cosmo_field_content.py, r4_11_a0z_2026-10-29.py, R4_23_EMERGENT_GR_2026-10-29.md, R4_29_DEFECT_FIELDS_2026-10-29.md find no lattice-to-FRW map, no embedding statement, no site-creation statement. Nearest neighbours are not the map: R4-23 leaves the strain-to-metric map OPEN (brief :74), R4-29 lists the curvature–metric map as missing item (iv) (brief :106) — both are metric-from-strain maps, not site-to-comoving-coordinate maps. E1: χ(n) z-independent; J_com constant = a_lat,com³/4; J_phys grows as A³; one fixed bijection serves all z. E2: χ(n, z) = r(n)/A(z) z-dependent; J_com(z) = (a_lat³/4)/A(z)³, falling as A^-3, while J_phys is constant; the index set grows with z, so Φ_z is a growing family of maps, not one fixed bijection. (2) SPACING FUNCTION — FORM: a_lat,phys(z) (equivalently a_lat,com(z)) for all z from z_f to today, plus which lattice quantity is held fixed. E1 form: a_lat,phys(z) = a_lat,com·A(z), a_lat,com constant. E2 form: a_lat,phys(z) = constant, independent of z and A. INVENTORY: NO SUCH FUNCTION EXISTS — one spacing value with no z-dependence stated anywhere (route4_step1_scales.py:12 C44, :14 spacing, :18 E0 = C44·a³; cosmo_field_content.py carries the same single spacing). R4-11's H(z) is a function of z for a0, not for a_lat (script :24–25, IMPORTED flat ΛCDM, brief grade UNTESTED at R4_11_A0Z_2026-10-29.md:3). E1: a_lat,phys scales with A; comoving spacing constant; physical site density falls as A^-3; comoving site density constant. E2: a_lat,phys a fixed physical constant; comoving spacing shrinks as A^-1; physical site density constant; comoving site density grows as A³. (3) U_LAT BEHAVIOUR — FORM: u_lat,phys(z) and u_lat,com(z) = A(z)³·u_lat,phys(z), plus a constitutive choice resolving the trilemma in u_lat = C44 = E0/a_lat,phys³: when a_lat,phys changes, C44, E0 per cell, and energy per site cannot all three stay fixed (C44 fixed ⇒ E0 grows as a_lat,phys³; E0 fixed ⇒ C44 falls as a_lat,phys^-3). The embedding must state which is held fixed (or give C44(z)/E0(z) directly). Energy density follows number density only if energy per site is fixed. INVENTORY: NO u_lat(z) EXISTS — C44 is a single value (route4_step1_scales.py:12); no C44(z) or E0(z) stated. The gravitating assumption is not a u_lat(z) law: uniform lattice vacuum does not gravitate as matter (cosmo_field_content.py:19,40), while a defect's energy is ASSUMED to gravitate as dust (same file :54–56, Assumption A1). E1 BRANCHED — E1 alone does not fix u_lat behaviour: number density always falls as A^-3, but physical energy density is CONSTANT on the C44-fixed branch (energy per site grows as A³, cancelling count dilution; u_lat,com grows as A³) and falls as A^-3 on the E0-fixed branch (energy per site fixed; u_lat,com constant). 'u_lat dilutes with expansion' is therefore ambiguous until the sub-branch is stated: true on E0-fixed, false on C44-fixed, for physical energy density; comoving energy density behaves oppositely. E2: the trilemma dissolves — a_lat fixed, so C44 and E0 can both stay fixed consistently. u_lat,phys is a FIXED physical energy density; u_lat,com grows as A³; total lattice energy in a comoving patch grows as A³, so the energy source for new-site lattice energy is part of the site-creation derivation (D3), not assumed. (4) COMOVING-NUMBER DEFINITION — FORM: (i) n_def,com(z) = N_def(z)/V_com(z), V_com = V_phys/A³; (ii) x(z) = N_def(z)/N_sites(z); (iii) identity n_def,com = x·(N_sites/V_com); (iv) a conservation statement naming WHICH quantity is conserved absent survival/creation (K_surv kept separate, not folded into the definition). Per comoving volume ≠ per site; they coincide only when N_sites per comoving volume is constant. INVENTORY: NO LATTICE DEFINITION EXISTS — cosmo_field_content.py:65 is a different candidate's required comoving number density today (from a target density and a defect mass), states no conservation law for this lattice, and is NOT imported. The comoving Jeans-scale line (:140) likewise defines no defect number. E1: N_sites per comoving volume constant, so x-conservation and n_def,com-conservation are the SAME statement (modulo K_surv). Row 256's conditional K_dil has a home here: its 'IF number per comoving volume conserved' clause is exactly this statement. E2: N_sites per comoving volume grows as A³, so the two conservation statements SPLIT and cannot both hold: x-conservation ⇒ n_def,com grows as A³; n_def,com-conservation ⇒ x falls as A^-3. Geometry alone conserves neither in general; the definition needs the site-creation rule (D3). (5) EXPANSION HISTORY — FORM: (i) A(z), or H(z) plus the A0 = 1 normalisation, over z_f to today, provenance labelled CPQR-derived or IMPORT; (ii) the thermal history T(A) (entropy/equation-of-state evolution) converting T_f to A_f and hence z_f. A(z) = 1/(1+z) alone is the definition of z and supplies no dynamics; the content is H(z) and T(A). INVENTORY: NO CPQR EXPANSION HISTORY EXISTS — the only H(z) is R4-11's IMPORTED flat ΛCDM form (script :24–25, matter fraction carried as 0.315, brief :3 UNTESTED), for an a0 test — usable only as labelled IMPORT, never as a CPQR result. No T(A) in inventory. The CPQR route is blocked at its source: R4-23 BLOCKED, no nonlinear GR, no lattice G, no FRW metric derived (brief :3, :41, D1 FAIL); R4-29 metric sector open/scope-banned (brief :6, :106). E1: A(z) needed to evaluate the dilution factor and convert comoving to physical spacing; with IMPORTED A(z) the E1 forms are evaluable as IMPORT, not CPQR. T(A) still needed for the value. E2: A(z) alone is INSUFFICIENT even as IMPORT: the E2 transfer additionally needs dN_site/dA and x_new(A) (D3). Without a creation rule the E2 K_dil form is underdetermined, whereas under E1, A(z) determines the defect-density factor once K_surv and the content-3 branch are fixed. E1/E2 CONSEQUENCES FOR K_DIL (key clarification of row 256's factor names, not a change to them): with today's u_lat,0 in K_crit, the symbolic transfer is Ω = R_vol(T_f)·K_crit,0·K_dil,total·K_surv·K_energy (NOT evaluated), where K_dil,total = (u_def,0/u_def,f)·(u_lat,f/u_lat,0) = (defect-density ratio)·K_u, and K_u = u_lat,f/u_lat,0 is the u_lat epoch ratio supplied by K_emb (content 3). Row 256's (1+z_f)^-3 is the DEFECT-DENSITY RATIO under comoving conservation with dust behaviour (energy per defect fixed, w = 0 assumed not derived). It equals K_dil,total only where K_u is unity. E1 defect-density ratio: (1+z_f)^-3, CONDITIONAL as in row 256 (n_def,com conserved modulo K_surv; energy per defect fixed; dust behaviour). E1 TOTAL BY SUB-BRANCH: E1-C44-fixed, K_u is unity ⇒ K_dil,total = (1+z_f)^-3 (row-256 form survives intact). E1-E0-fixed, K_u = (1+z_f)^3 ⇒ K_dil,total is unity (times K_surv, K_energy): the defect-density dilution is exactly cancelled by the u_lat epoch ratio, because R_vol is a ratio of two energy densities that both dilute as A^-3 on this branch. So E1 alone does NOT settle K_dil,total; E1 plus the constitutive branch does. E2 defect-density ratio: NEEDING DERIVATION — no single closed form follows from the E2 definition alone. Exactly what derivation is needed (D3): the site-creation rule, i.e. x_new(A) (defect fraction among newly created sites vs A) and dN_site/dA (equivalently N_sites(A)), plus survival of pre-existing defects and energy per defect vs A. General form once D3 exists: N_def(A) = N_def,f + ∫ from A_f to A0 of [x_new(A′)·dN_site/dA′ − losses(A′)] dA′; ratio = (N_def,0/N_def,f)·(A_f/A0)³·(E_def,0/E_def,f). E2 K_u is unity (u_lat,phys fixed), so E2's K_dil,total equals its defect-density ratio once D3 is known. CONDITIONAL TEMPLATES (forms, not derivations, none selected): E2a, new sites defect-free, pre-existing N_def conserved, energy per defect fixed: ratio = (1+z_f)^-3 — same FORM as E1-C44 but for a different reason (absolute N_def conserved while V_phys grows; n_def,com conserved, x falls as (1+z_f)^-3). E2b, new sites created at the formation fraction x_f: N_def grows with N_sites, n_def,phys constant, ratio = unity; x conserved, n_def,com grows as A³. E2c, general x_new(A): the integral form above; not constrained to the E2a–E2b interval by definition alone. SUMMARY (exponents of (1+z_f) in K_dil,total, K_surv/K_energy factored out): E1-C44-fixed: −3. E1-E0-fixed: 0. E2a: −3. E2b: 0. E2 general: no single exponent; integral form, NEEDING DERIVATION (D3). DECISION OBJECT (NOT SELECTED — what would decide E1 vs E2): D1 — a derived CPQR expansion history plus a lattice-dynamics result for uniform dilation: does lattice spacing track A (a breathing mode following expansion, E1) or stay fixed by microphysics (E2)? Needs the missing lattice action whose variation would yield a metric field equation (R4-23's blocking step), or an equivalent uniform-dilation-mode result. An IMPORTED ΛCDM A(z) cannot decide this (it assumes a comoving frame for matter, not a lattice). D2 — a constitutive derivation under uniform dilation: does C44 stay fixed or does E0 per cell stay fixed? Decides the E1 sub-branch and hence K_u. D3 — if E2, a site-creation rule: what process creates new sites, at what rate dN_site/dA, with what defect fraction x_new(A) among newly created sites, plus the energy source for new-site lattice energy. GRADE NOTE (coordinator judgment, stated plainly): FORMULATION-COMPLETE accepted because the brief expressly allowed E2's changed form to be stated OR marked as needing derivation, and the worker marked it NEEDING DERIVATION with the exact derivation (D3) plus conditional templates — that satisfies the pre-registered bar. If 'NEEDING DERIVATION' for E2's closed form is read as itself a PARTIAL, the downgrade is mechanical: change only the grade string and grade reason; no content, consequence, or check changes. What is NOT complete, under either grade, is selection (E1 vs E2), the content-3 constitutive branch, the E2 creation rule, and all values (K_crit, K_dil, K_surv, T_f, T(A)) — those remain open decisions/values, not hidden defaults. Scripts theory/kemb_embedding_contents_batch28_2026-10-30.py (+ verify 124 PASS, 0 FAIL on coordinator re-run: own exponent/Jacobian recomputation, re-grep of all 16 cited file+line traces, re-run of 16 absence greps, barred-token absence scan over all four output files with dynamically built tokens — zero matches, no-numeric-Ω check, no z_f/T_f/epoch numeric-assignment check, no unity-default check, no heavy-compute tokens, no ledger writes; .json, brief KEMB_EMBEDDING_CONTENTS_BATCH28_2026-10-30.md). Runtime two pure-Python runs, sequential, well under a second total (verifier measured 0.176 s). No rows re-graded.

Curve quantities: λ* = argmin over interval I, κ = second derivative at λ*, cost ΔE(λ) = E(λ) - E(λ*), truncation scale ε_trunc. Held-fixed variants (trilemma resolved explicitly): H_E0 — E0 per cell fixed: E_phys(λ) = E0·E_red(λ). H_C44 — C44 fixed: E_phys(λ) = λ³·E0,1·E_red(λ); the λ³ weighting can shift λ*/κ relative to H_E0. H_SITE — energy per site fixed: IDENTICAL to H_E0 by E_site = u_lat·a³/4 = E0/4 (not a third distinct curve). H_BOTH — C44 + E0 fixed together: no curve exists, by contradiction (a³ = E0/C44 constant, λ = 1 only). Decision rule (constitutive leg only), stated in advance: R (restoring, branch-discriminating): λ* = 1 within resolution, κ > 0 resolved, ΔE grows away from λ* under H_E0, AND the H_C44 curve differs from H_E0 by more than resolution -> E2 constitutive leg passes. F (no preferred spacing): no interior minimum in I with variation across I much larger than ε_trunc (resolved drift) -> E2 constitutive leg fails, E1 constitutive leg passes on this leg only. M (moved minimum): λ* ≠ 1 by more than resolution under H_E0 -> diagnostic for bond law/reference; D1 inconclusive. INCONCLUSIVE (a): minimum at current spacing under BOTH branches with no discrimination -> inconclusive for D1 (calibration null), not evidence for tracking. INCONCLUSIVE (b): curve flat to within its own truncation error -> inconclusive, not evidence for a fixed spacing. Run plan COSTED, NOT EXECUTED: fixed row-227/MEP pair list (48,380 pairs), scale coordinates, sum V per λ, variant weighting, per-site normalisation; ~10^6 pair evaluations (pairs × order-ten-to-twenty λ points), seconds-scale, pure bond sums — anchored on the same-order MEP total-pair-sum profile already in inventory. Survey verdict — NOTHING already is a dilation-mode result in disguise (all NO, with traces): static K44 (shear, volume-preserving) and phonon K44 (fixed-volume transverse slope) are shear moduli, not dilation derivatives; prestress relation (p = -3.0566, static-inferred = ρv² + p/2) is a single-point fixed-volume shear correction, not a curve — at most a point derivative at λ = 1, giving no λ*, κ, or A comparison; closest fragment is batch 18's NN-only first derivative at zero strain, which vanishes at the perfect lattice (V′(0) = 0) for a subset of pairs — a zero point derivative, not a curve; Morse bulk curvatures sit at their own model's minimum (tautological λ* = 1) under a different law; MEP scans are endpoint-interpolation paths, never E(λ); no E(λ) curve and no a_lat(z)/C44(z) function exists outside the row-258 formulation. Circularity guard: λ is a lattice variable (λ = 1 at inventory spacing); A never enters the E(λ) computation, only afterwards in the decision rule's comparison, as a labelled import or derived history; an imported ΛCDM A(z) cannot decide E1 vs E2 (it assumes a comoving frame for matter, not a lattice). COUPLING LEG — exact missing object (named, not supplied): the lattice action whose variation yields a metric field equation (R4-23's blocking step), plus the λ-A coupling term and the expansion driving energy scale. E(λ) alone supplies the constitutive leg; without the coupling leg, D1 as a whole stays open whatever E(λ) returns, and a constitutive outcome must not be presented as a D1 decision on its own.
