| 272: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | BOX-DRIFT FIRST-EIGENSOLVE COST BOUNDS CHECK — UNJUDGEABLE (SLOW VS HUNG NOT DECIDABLE FROM INVENTORY) (older-backlog batch 35 thread 2 of 2, row 270 recommendation 3 follow-up; ledger infrastructure) | READ-ONLY analysis: no eigensolve run, PID 2007 untouched, no script edits, no grading, no launches. Pre-registered in analysis header before analysis: scaling model in form; L=10 data points with sources; verdict mapping (within predicted range -> EXPECTED-SLOW; >2x the top of a JUSTIFIED predicted range -> HUNG-SUSPECT; no justified finite top -> UNJUDGEABLE + resolving measurement). Scaling model (read from the scripts, not assumed): N (91/127/217/271) is NOT the eigensolve dimension — it is the atom/geometry count, entering only via field build (excluded from point times) and via the spectrum (gap, unknown). Dimension is set by grid M: L=10 M=36 -> 4*36^3 = 186,624 (loop_zavg.py:66); L=14 M=50 -> 4*50^3 = 500,000 (loop_3dfine_L14.py); dimension ratio (50/36)^3 = 2.679. No sparse matrix is ever formed (jr_operator.py header): lowest_abs = scipy eigsh on H^2, matrix-free, k=4, which='SA', tol=1e-7; each ARPACK iteration costs 2 matvecs (FFT pair + 16*M^3 K-kernel einsum + O(M^3) real-space terms); per-matvec factor L=10->L=14: [2.679, 2.897] (M^3 to M^3*log M^3). ARPACK iteration count is NOT assumed N-, M-, or g-independent: it depends on the H^2 gap near zero at tol=1e-7, and the L=10 data at fixed dimension already span 38 s -> 2005 s (53x) from g alone. Field build excluded everywhere (L=14 builds 130-332 s, median ~137 s; L=10 builds 20-120 s); the ~2h50m is post-"fields ready" eigensolve time. Point 1 is cold in both cases (L=14 v0=None; L=10 logs show the warm-start fallback to cold on every later point), so cold-vs-cold holds. L=10 per-point eigensolve times (seconds; verifier re-grepped every value): N=91: 42, 51, 62, 85, 85, 96, 108, 250, 474, 521, 845 (loop_3dfine_N91.log, 11 pts, ends 11/14); N=127: 62, 156, 169, 272, 248, 326, 367, 787, 902 (loop_3dfine_N127{,_cont,_cont2}.log, 9 pts); N=217: 71, 171, 139, 222, 299, 298, 403, 672, 2005 (loop_3dfine_N217_run.log, 9 pts); N=271: 38, 83, 109, 132, 160, 170, 228, 330, 575, 416 (loop_3dfine_N271_run.log, 10 pts). ABSENT (a result, not a gap to fill): N=271 L=10 at g=-1.30 — the exact analogue of the L=14 first point — was never computed; no L=14 point time, iteration count, matvec count, or E(L=14, -1.30) exists, so its H^2 gap is unmeasured; L=10 E values oscillate across the crossing (N=91: -0.0162/+0.0195/-0.0151 at -1.15/-1.20/-1.30), so E(L=14, -1.30) cannot be extrapolated. Predicted ranges for the L=14 N=271 first eigensolve (g=-1.30): closest-analogue (416-845 s) x [2.679, 2.897] = 1115-2448 s (19-41 min); near-crossing dive spread (|E|<=0.04: 108-2005 s) x 2.897 top = 289-5808 s (up to 97 min). Observed (dead chain: launch loop_3dfine_L14_run.log:147 2026-10-30T00:55:47Z, fields ready 155 s at line 157, VM restart 23:46 EDT Oct 29): ~10,080 s (2h48m; dispatch rounds to ~2h50m). Verdict: UNJUDGEABLE. Observed/top = 4.12x the closest-analogue top (would breach the 2x HUNG-SUSPECT threshold IF that top were justified) but 1.74x the dive-spread top (under the threshold, i.e. EXPECTED-SLOW-borderline IF that top were justified). Both tops smuggle in an assumed gap: the first assumes the L=14 gap is no smaller than the L=10 dive gaps; the second assumes it is no smaller than the worst L=10 dive gap. Neither is justified by any data, and the verdict flips between them — that flip is itself the evidence the scaling is too uncertain to judge. This is NOT evidence of a hang, and NOT evidence of mere slowness. Note also: zero `L14 g=` point lines and zero `L14 DONE` lines exist across all 12 launches, consistent with a point longer than any single chain's lifetime between restarts, not only with a hang. Resolving measurements (follow-ups, NOT executed here): (1) immediate, read-only: coordinator checks the live chain's CPU (ps / /proc/<pid>/stat) — ~100% of one core sustained => ARPACK is computing (slow, not hung); ~0% sustained => hung/IO-wait; (2) standalone instrumented probe (after the current chain resolves or dies, never alongside it): rebuild/load the L=14 M=50 N=271 fields (~140 s), construct DiracOperator(M=50) at g=-1.30, run lowest_abs(k=4, tol=1e-7) with a matvec counter and a heartbeat print every 60 s — separates per-matvec seconds (validates 2.68-2.90x) vs ARPACK iteration count at the true L=14 gap (the unknown); detached, capped walltime, no grading of its E value. Catches fixed in-thread (worker report): dive-subset header initially omitted two |E|<=0.04 points (N=91 108 s, N=127 326 s); corrected to the uniform |E|<=0.04 definition (15 dive points, 108-2005 s) and all derived tops/ratios recomputed (2448 s / 5808 s); verifier check 28 initially failed on prose mentions of jr_operator in the analysis docstring; fixed the verifier to test real imports/calls. | 274: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | CDM g STRONGER-EXCLUSION OPTIONS FORMULATION FOR THE CONTINUUM LEMMA — FORMULATION-COMPLETE (FORM ONLY; NO RUN, NO RULE SELECTION, LEMMA NOT PROVED) (older-backlog batch 36 thread 2 of 2, row 271 follow-up; ledger CDM. g-continuum) | Pre-registered in script header before formulation: bar (both options stated in form with validity, expected gain in form, cost in form, failure modes + fallbacks; no constant fitted; no covering run executed; no option selected) and the binding 'does NOT do' list (does not run either option, does not claim the lemma, does not select between anisotropic, second-order, or continued isotropic subdivision); verifier checks the header position and the OPEN selection. Background read first: interval_cover_v7_batch35_2026-10-30.py and mean_value_exclusion_batch31_2026-10-30.py + JSON (the batch-31 .md was not on disk; the .py/.json carried the full MV form). (a) ANISOTROPIC SUBDIVISION — rule in form: bisect a failing box only along axis set S (|S| = k, 2^k children), splitting [lo_b, hi_b] at its midpoint for b in S; axis choice via near-exclusion score n_a = 2|g_a|/W_a in [0,1) per failing component, splitting along the top width-contributor axis of the most nearly excluding component; default map 1 failing component -> k=1, 2 -> k=1 (same top axis) or 2, 3 -> comparability fallback. Validity (tiling, explicit): children are the 2^k split/whole interval combinations; per split axis [lo,hi] = [lo,m] u [m,hi] exactly, interiors disjoint (shared face only), so the product union is exactly the parent; the coverage check must be rewritten — per-parent child count = 2^|S| (not 8), per-axis interval tiling verified with the SAME midpoint value for both children, every child carrying its own edge vector and split-count vector, global coverage by induction over the subdivision forest; naive/MV rules already hold for rectangular boxes. Gain in form: per round, saving vs isotropic 8 = 8/2^k — 1-axis -> 4x, 2-axis -> 2x, 3-axis -> 1x (degenerate); total leaf saving 2^(3d-(n_x+n_y+n_z)) holds only for a fixed isotropic depth d and resolution vector, realised only for axes never split on a lineage and deferred (not avoided) if an unsplit axis is split later — never more leaves than isotropic for the same final resolution vector, but possibly more rounds; per-box cost unchanged in form. Depth/edge bookkeeping: scalar depth breaks (depth d admits any edge vector with n_x+n_y+n_z = d); boxes must store lo + edge vector + split-count vector; caps must be per-axis and/or minimum-edge, not scalar depth; the residue format's scalar 'edge' field must be extended to a vector. Failure mode + fallbacks: all three components comparably failing -> axis choice carries no information, lineage elongates (aspect ratio 2^k) and widths barely shrink; fallbacks: comparably failing iff n_(1)-n_(3) <= theta (theta a symbolic pre-registration parameter; illustrative theta = 0.25, NOT fitted, NOT adopted) -> full 8-way; aspect ratio > R_cap (symbolic; illustrative 8, not fitted, not adopted) -> full 8-way; no-progress guard (failing child reuses union of parent's and child's top axes) -> S reaches k=3 within 3 rounds; worst case = isotropic + bounded extra rounds. (b) SECOND-ORDER TAYLOR FORM — formula in form: grad(r) in grad(c) + H(c).D + R2,int, R2,int,a = (1/2) sum_{b,c} T_int,abc . D_b . D_c (D_b.D_c = [-h_b h_c/4, +h_b h_c/4] for b != c; [0, h_b^2/4] for b = c). Per pair, T_{i,abk} = S.[V'''_i.u_a u_b u_k + (A_i/L_i).(delta_ak u_b + delta_bk u_a + delta_ab u_k - 3 u_a u_b u_k)] — the bracket is the tangential term (derivatives of V'/L and of direction cosines) and must not be dropped. V'''(d) = -3*sqrt(2) + 24d is linear, so its interval is exact endpoints; A_i/L_i and u quotients valid iff Llo_i > 0; full symmetry -> 10 unique triples (not 27); SO-EXCLUDED iff some component excludes zero. Validity: Taylor with integral remainder (exact; integral_0^1 (1-t) dt = 1/2); segment c->r lies in the box (convex), so enclosing T over B encloses it over every segment; smoothness on B iff Llo_i > 0 for all pairs (checked per box); T_int enclosure from batch-31 interval steps (same-source factors treated independently widen only); point grad(c), H(c) under the H4 stated-margin status (EPS_ROUND = 1e-6 MeV outward widening, not a proved bound); straddling boxes handled as MV does (centre need not be in the remainder; box not classified as in-remainder). When it beats MV (form): MV excess = (Hessian-interval width) x edge; SO excess = (third-derivative bound) x edge^2, width(R2) ~ C_T.h^2; SO narrower iff width(R2,a) < E_MV,a. Crossover h* = H_scale/L: 1840.17/130993.46 = 0.014048, 2500/130993.46 = 0.019085, 1840.17/140000 = 0.013144, 2500/140000 = 0.017857 — h* ~ 0.013-0.019. At depth-11 edge h = 0.00009765625, crude SO excess L.h^2 ~ 0.00125 MeV vs crude MV excess H_scale.h ~ 0.18-0.24 MeV (ratio h*/h ~ 144x). FLAGGED APPROXIMATION: the crude crossover treats width(H_int) as order H_scale (dependency-dominated); if H_int is Lipschitz-tight, width(H_int) ~ L.sqrt(3).h and E_MV is also O(h^2), so the honest asymptotic comparison is a coefficient comparison, not an order crossover, and h* is not a proved threshold — it marks where L.h^2 becomes comparable to H_scale.h. The ~144x ratio is a scale illustration, not a predicted gain; realised gain per box is (E_MV,a - width(R2,a))/2 in half-width. Cost in form (estimate, not measured): SO adds per pair a V''' interval (cheap) + (A/L) quotient + 10 triple-product intervals, sharing L/u/V''/T/A with MV; contraction 18 interval products + cheap point H(c); estimated ~1.5-2.5x MV = 380-1010 us/box (from adopted MV 252-405 us), i.e. ~3.5-9x naive ~110 us; staged form: SO only on boxes failing both naive and MV, so incremental cost is bounded by the MV-failing residue size x SO per-box cost. Failure modes: (F1) singular (any Llo <= 0) -> whole form FAILS, never excludes, one pair poisons all entries; (F2) wide T_int near a host (u width ~ h/Llo, A/L large, triple products widen) or for large boxes -> SO fails where MV succeeds, correctly inconclusive; (F3) boxes at r* (grad(c) small) still fail — excision + subdivision remain the handling, gain only in a thin shell near the MV threshold; (F4) dependency excess in T_int itself; (F5) unstaged full-wave SO would dominate the budget far from r* — guarded by staging. Obstructions: none for either option. Not established here (and not claimed): any numeric gain, any per-box width comparison on real boxes, any rounding bound, any selection. Coordinator re-ran worker verifier: 29/29 PASS (crossover recomputation, saving factors, entry counts, barred-content scan on executable code only; verifier initially failed 3/29 on its own scan bugs — title-line truncation and prohibition phrases in the docstring header counted as violations — formulation unchanged, verifier fixed to use the standalone section header and scan executable code only, then 29/29). Selection remains OPEN for a future run's pre-registration decision. |

(ii) Dominant-pair gradient lower bound — split grad E = G_D + G_R; if inf_Omega |G_D.w| > sup_Omega ||G_R|| uniformly, then grad E != 0 on Omega; pointwise gaps do not suffice; any gap must survive near-r* cancellation (pair gradients cancel at r*); no D or w chosen; pair data from this thread (mean |S.V'| over centres): 2876 (466.5) > 2875 (228.2) > 2874 (206.5) > 8602 (185.9) > 8597 (169.3) > 2880 (162.6) > 3653 (157.2) > 2937 (155.9) MeV/unit; 2876 also dominates mean energy (61.6 MeV) — data for choosing D, not a choice. (iii) Degree/index (formulated only, NOT claimed to apply) — deg(grad E, int K, 0) = sum of interior zero indices; index = sign(det H); r* index +1 IF H(r*) positive definite; uniqueness needs deg = +1 AND det H > 0 throughout K; degree = +1 alone does NOT imply uniqueness (extra +1/-1 zeros can cancel) — fallacy explicitly named; still needed: verified grad E != 0 on every boundary piece (Omega's boundary is non-smooth), the degree value, det H > 0 on Omega, H(r*) positive definite; none computed here. (iv) Ray monotonicity from r* — phi_u(t) = E(r* + t*u) strictly increasing on {t > 0 : r* + t*u in Omega}, equivalently radial derivative > 0 throughout Omega; would imply the lemma; falsified by any in-Omega radial-derivative <= 0 or genuine in-Omega sampled decrease. Falsifying rays 81 (123 falsifying steps); out-of-domain-decrease-only rays 17 (17 steps). Falsifying drops 0.45-12.59 MeV (exact range 0.4542-12.5930; smallest is 45x the 0.01 MeV tolerance, so not tolerance-sensitive); by step 0.5->0.75: 4, 0.75->1.0: 43, 1.0->1.25: 38, 1.25->1.5: 38; radius range t = 0.286-0.629. STRATEGY (iv) AS STATED IS FALSIFIED (81 genuine in-Omega decreasing rays). What LIVE genuinely establishes is that the operative margin for a rigorous Krawczyk bound is in r-space (Newton distances about 1e-9, diagnostic widening 1e-12, float errors about 1e-16): a proved r-space rounding bound of, say, 1e-13 would preserve all 500 exclusions, whereas MV's 1e-6 grad margin is precisely what keeps boxes alive. | 330: | coordinator 2026-10-30: | CDM PER-MINIMUM K_SURV FORMULATION — FORMULATION-COMPLETE (no K_surv value, no rate, no Omega evaluation) (backlog batch 74; ledger CDM) | FIRST FINDING: row 256 defines K_surv only as a gloss — 'net post-freeze-out survival/creation per comoving volume (S_net) — MISSING' — no criterion, no form, no per-minimum split anywhere in rows 256/258/260/262/288, so the definition here is PROPOSED, not inventory. FORM: transfer matrix M_ij (fraction born in j ending in i; late occupation x_i^late = SUM_j M_ij x_j^birth); K_surv,i = M_ii (diagonal survival: stays in i — a redistribution factor, NOT total survival); row-256 net factor K_surv^net = SUM_ij M_ij p_j^birth (reduces to SUM_i K_surv,i p_i^birth in the diagonal limit). CONSERVATION: within the row-227 position model SUM_i M_ij = 1 (no annihilation channel), so any K_surv^net != 1 needs an OUT-OF-MODEL channel (MISSING) — barrier heights alone can never determine the net factor. FREEZE-OUT RULE: at full equilibrium the occupation is batch 72's form regardless of barriers; K_surv,i != 1 arises ONLY from non-equilibrium history; treating K_surv,i as an equilibrium factor is wrong. RATES: k_i(T) = SUM_s nu_s exp(-B_{i,s}/kT), B PINNED frozen (batch 73); nu_s MISSING (needs row-253 dynamical mass — Hessian eigenvalues pinned in batch 69 do not suffice), so no rate quoted even in scale. Smallest-barrier form k_i ~ nu_dom exp(-B_i/kT) holds ONLY when every non-dominant channel has DeltaB >> kT — it FAILS for r* and 1377/0 at illustrative kT = 10 MeV even at equal nu (multi-channel ratios 2.5428, 2.2502; 28760/2 1.0802, 7634/2 1.0902, 37996/1 exactly 1.0000 single channel; kT = 100: 3.9434 / 2.9022 / 2.2160 / 2.0210 / 1.0000; nu ratios MISSING, so ratios are ILLUSTRATIVE of channel structure, not rates). SMALLEST BARRIERS (MeV, frozen): r* 40.5014 via 31410/7, 37996/1 15.6754 via 36727/7 (single channel), 28760/2 5.2751 via 31410/7, 7634/2 6.3624 via 14111/2, 1377/0 8.5895 via 12366/0. PATH RULE: a path's effective barrier = max saddle energy along the path minus E_start, NOT the sum; brute-force over all simple paths: cheapest path to r* is DIRECT for every minimum (same barriers as above) — no multi-step path beats any direct barrier. SURVIVAL OVER HISTORY: K_surv,i = exp(-INT k_i(T(t)) dt), equivalently over T with |dT/dt|; history T(t) MISSING (D1, rows 258/288), last-equilibrium T_f MISSING (row 233 O1). FROZEN LIMIT: x_i^late = K_surv,i x_i^eq(T_f) ONLY if populations keep their birth distribution (k_i t_history << 1); it fails under partial equilibration — the general form is the master equation dP_i/dt = SUM_j (k_{j->i} P_j - k_{i->j} P_i) with k_{i->j} from batch-73 barriers (nu MISSING); K_surv,i is its frozen-limit special case. BRANCH: all barriers frozen; relaxed-branch barriers OPEN (35.6 MeV r* gap does NOT transfer); same-branch minima + saddles required for any evaluation. REMAINING MISSING: nu_s, history, T_f, relaxed minima/saddles, annihilation/creation channel, K_energy conditional generalisation inputs; Omega NOT EVALUATED. Primary 44/44 PASS, verifier 32/32 PASS (Decimal barriers, brute-force paths, independent ratios). Scripts: theory/ksurv_batch74_2026-10-30.py + .json, theory/ksurv_batch74_verify_2026-10-30.py + .json; brief theory/ksurv_batch74_2026-10-30.md. No Discord post, no GitHub push (local index only). |
